<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 245. DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.001"><b>245.001</b> MS 2006 [Renumbered 15.001]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.01"><b>245.01</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/593/#laws.0.6.0">1953 c 593 s 6</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.02"><b>245.02</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/593/#laws.0.6.0">1953 c 593 s 6</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.021">
        <h1 class="shn">245.021 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <p>(a) For the purposes of this chapter, the definitions in this section have the meanings given them.</p>
        <p>(b) "Commissioner" means the commissioner of human services.</p>
        <p>(c) "Executive board" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.015">246C.015</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.8.1.0">2024 c 79 art 8 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.03">
        <h1 class="shn">245.03 DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES ESTABLISHED; COMMISSIONER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.03.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>There is created a Department of Human Services. A commissioner of human services shall be appointed by the governor under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.06">15.06</a>. The commissioner shall be selected on the basis of ability and experience in welfare and without regard to political affiliations. The commissioner may appoint up to two deputy commissioners.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.03.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Mission; efficiency.</span></h2>
          <p>It is part of the department's mission that within the department's resources the commissioner shall endeavor to:</p>
          <p>(1) prevent the waste or unnecessary spending of public money;</p>
          <p>(2) use innovative fiscal and human resource practices to manage the state's resources and operate the department as efficiently as possible, including the authority to consolidate different nonentitlement grant programs, having similar functions or serving similar populations, as may be determined by the commissioner, while protecting the original purposes of the programs. Nonentitlement grant funds consolidated by the commissioner shall be reflected in the department's biennial budget. With approval of the commissioner, vendors who are eligible for funding from any of the commissioner's granting authority under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.01#stat.256.01.2">256.01, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (6), may submit a single application for a grant agreement including multiple awards;</p>
          <p>(3) coordinate the department's activities wherever appropriate with the activities of other governmental agencies;</p>
          <p>(4) use technology where appropriate to increase agency productivity, improve customer service, increase public access to information about government, and increase public participation in the business of government;</p>
          <p>(5) utilize constructive and cooperative labor-management practices to the extent otherwise required by chapters 43A and 179A;</p>
          <p>(6) report to the legislature on the performance of agency operations and the accomplishment of agency goals in the agency's biennial budget according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.10#stat.16A.10.1">16A.10, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p>(7) recommend to the legislature appropriate changes in law necessary to carry out the mission and improve the performance of the department.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/593/#laws.0.1.0">1953 c 593 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/45/#laws.0.17.0">1965 c 45 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1129/#laws.8.6.0">1969 c 1129 art 8 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/305/#laws.0.30.0">1977 c 305 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.5.58.0">1984 c 654 art 5 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/248/#laws.11.18.0">1995 c 248 art 11 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/203/#laws.9.2.0">1997 c 203 art 9 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/366/#laws.0.64.0">1998 c 366 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.6.1.0">2013 c 108 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.61.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 61</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.031"><b>245.031</b> [Obsolete]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.0311"><b>245.0311</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.3.18.0">2014 c 262 art 3 s 18</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.0312"><b>245.0312</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.3.18.0">2014 c 262 art 3 s 18</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.0313">
        <h1 class="shn">245.0313 AID TO PERSONS WITH DISABILITIES.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding any provision of law to the contrary, the cost of care not met by federal funds for any developmentally disabled patient eligible for the medical assistance program or the Supplemental Security Income for the aged, blind and disabled program in institutions under the control of the commissioner of human services shall be paid by the state and county in the same proportion as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.19">256B.19</a> for division of costs.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.0313.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1136/#laws.0.23.0">1969 c 1136 s 23</a> subd 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/961/#laws.0.20.0">1971 c 961 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/717/#laws.0.10.0">1973 c 717 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/360/#laws.2.13.0">1981 c 360 art 2 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.5.58.0">1984 c 654 art 5 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.032"><b>245.032</b> [Obsolete]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.033"><b>245.033</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/717/#laws.0.33.0">1973 c 717 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.035">
        <h1 class="shn">245.035 INTERVIEW EXPENSES.</h1>
        <p>Job applicants for professional, administrative, or highly technical positions recruited by the commissioner of human services may be reimbursed for necessary travel expenses to and from interviews arranged by the commissioner of human services.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.035.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/163/#laws.0.42.0">1976 c 163 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.5.58.0">1984 c 654 art 5 s 58</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.036"><b>245.036</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.16#stat.246C.16.1">246C.16, subdivision 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.037"><b>245.037</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.16">246C.16, subd 2</a>]</div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.072"><b>245.072</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.4.9.0">2014 c 262 art 4 s 9</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.073">
        <h1 class="shn">245.073 TECHNICAL TRAINING; COMMUNITY-BASED PROGRAMS.</h1>
        <p>(a) In conjunction with the discharge of persons from regional treatment centers and their admission to state-operated and privately operated community-based programs, the commissioner may provide technical training assistance to the community-based programs. The commissioner may apply for and accept money from any source including reimbursement charges from the community-based programs for reasonable costs of training. Money received must be deposited in the general fund and is appropriated annually to the commissioner of human services for training under this section.</p>
        <p>(b) The commissioner must coordinate with the executive board to provide technical training assistance to community-based programs under this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.11#stat.246C.11.5">246C.11, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.073.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.6.2.0">1989 c 282 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.10.2.0">2024 c 79 art 10 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.08"><b>245.08</b> [Obsolete]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.09"><b>245.09</b> [Unnecessary]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.095">
        <h1 class="shn">245.095 LIMITS ON RECEIVING PUBLIC FUNDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.095.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a provider, vendor, or individual enrolled, licensed, receiving funds under a grant contract, or registered in any program administered by the commissioner, including under the commissioner's powers and authorities in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.01">256.01</a>, is excluded from that program, the commissioner shall:</p>
          <p>(1) prohibit the excluded provider, vendor, or individual from enrolling, becoming licensed, receiving grant funds, or registering in any other program administered by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(2) disenroll, revoke or suspend a license, disqualify, or debar the excluded provider, vendor, or individual in any other program administered by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) If a provider, vendor, or individual enrolled, licensed, receiving funds under a grant contract, or registered in any program administered by the commissioner, including under the commissioner's powers and authorities in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.01">256.01</a>, is excluded from that program, the commissioner may:</p>
          <p>(1) prohibit any associated entities or associated individuals from enrolling, becoming licensed, receiving grant funds, or registering in any other program administered by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(2) disenroll, revoke or suspend a license of, disqualify, or debar any associated entities or associated individuals in any other program administered by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) If a provider, vendor, or individual enrolled, licensed, or otherwise receiving funds under any contract or registered in any program administered by a Minnesota state or federal agency is excluded from that program, the commissioner of human services may:</p>
          <p>(1) prohibit the excluded provider, vendor, individual, or any associated entities or associated individuals from enrolling, becoming licensed, receiving grant funds, or registering in any program administered by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(2) disenroll, revoke or suspend a license of, disqualify, or debar the excluded provider, vendor, individual, or any associated entities or associated individuals in any program administered by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(d) The duration of a prohibition, disenrollment, revocation, suspension, disqualification, or debarment under paragraph (a) must last for the longest applicable sanction or disqualifying period in effect for the provider, vendor, or individual permitted by state or federal law. The duration of a prohibition, disenrollment, revocation, suspension, disqualification, or debarment under paragraphs (b) and (c) may last until up to the longest applicable sanction or disqualifying period in effect for the provider, vendor, individual, associated entity, or associated individual as permitted by state or federal law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.095.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following definitions have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Associated entity" means a provider or vendor owned or controlled by an excluded individual.</p>
          <p>(c) "Associated individual" means an individual or entity that has a relationship with the business or its owners or controlling individuals, such that the individual or entity would have knowledge of the financial practices of the program in question.</p>
          <p>(d) "Excluded" means removed under other authorities from a program administered by a Minnesota state or federal agency, including a final determination to stop payments.</p>
          <p>(e) "Individual" means a natural person providing products or services as a provider or vendor.</p>
          <p>(f) "Provider" means any entity, individual, owner, controlling individual, license holder, director, or managerial official of an entity receiving payment from a program administered by a Minnesota state or federal agency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.095.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Within five days of taking an action under subdivision (1), paragraph (a), (b), or (c), against a provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity, the commissioner must send notice of the action to the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity. The notice must state:</p>
          <p>(1) the basis for the action;</p>
          <p>(2) the effective date of the action;</p>
          <p>(3) the right to appeal the action; and</p>
          <p>(4) the requirements and procedures for reinstatement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.095.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Appeal.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon receipt of a notice under subdivision 3, a provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity may request a contested case hearing, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.02#stat.14.02.3">14.02, subdivision 3</a>, by filing with the commissioner a written request of appeal. The scope of any contested case hearing is solely limited to action taken under this section. The commissioner must receive the appeal request no later than 30 days after the date the notice was mailed to the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity. The appeal request must specify:</p>
          <p>(1) each disputed item and the reason for the dispute;</p>
          <p>(2) the authority in statute or rule upon which the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity relies for each disputed item;</p>
          <p>(3) the name and address of the person or entity with whom contacts may be made regarding the appeal; and</p>
          <p>(4) any other information required by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.095.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Withholding of payments.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as otherwise provided by state or federal law, the commissioner may withhold payments to a provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity in any program administered by the commissioner if the commissioner determines there is a credible allegation of fraud for which an investigation is pending for a program administered by a Minnesota state or federal agency.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, "credible allegation of fraud" means an allegation that has been verified by the commissioner from any source, including but not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) fraud hotline complaints;</p>
          <p>(2) claims data mining;</p>
          <p>(3) patterns identified through provider audits, civil false claims cases, and law enforcement investigations; and</p>
          <p>(4) court filings and other legal documents, including but not limited to police reports, complaints, indictments, informations, affidavits, declarations, and search warrants.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must send notice of the withholding of payments within five days of taking such action. The notice must:</p>
          <p>(1) state that payments are being withheld according to this subdivision;</p>
          <p>(2) set forth the general allegations related to the withholding action, except the notice need not disclose specific information concerning an ongoing investigation;</p>
          <p>(3) state that the withholding is for a temporary period and cite the circumstances under which the withholding will be terminated; and</p>
          <p>(4) inform the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity of the right to submit written evidence to contest the withholding action for consideration by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(d) If the commissioner withholds payments under this subdivision, the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity has a right to request administrative reconsideration. A request for administrative reconsideration must be made in writing, state with specificity the reasons the payment withholding decision is in error, and include documents to support the request. Within 60 days from receipt of the request, the commissioner shall judiciously review allegations, facts, evidence available to the commissioner, and information submitted by the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity to determine whether the payment withholding should remain in place.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner shall stop withholding payments if the commissioner determines there is insufficient evidence of fraud by the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity or when legal proceedings relating to the alleged fraud are completed, unless the commissioner has sent notice under subdivision 3 to the provider, vendor, individual, associated individual, or associated entity.</p>
          <p>(f) The withholding of payments is a temporary action and is not subject to appeal under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a> or chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.095.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.5.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.13.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.8.2.0">2023 c 70 art 8 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.096">
        <h1 class="shn">245.096 CHANGES TO GRANT PROGRAMS.</h1>
        <p>Prior to implementing any substantial changes to a grant funding formula disbursed through allocations administered by the commissioner, the commissioner must provide a report on the nature of the changes, the effect the changes will have, whether any funding will change, and other relevant information, to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees with jurisdiction over human services. The report must be provided prior to the start of a regular session, and the proposed changes cannot be implemented until after the adjournment of that regular session.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.096.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/99/#laws.1.5.0">2022 c 99 art 1 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.0962">
        <h1 class="shn">245.0962 QUALITY PARENTING INITIATIVE GRANT PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.0962.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services must establish a quality parenting initiative grant program to implement quality parenting initiative principles and practices to support children and families experiencing foster care placements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.0962.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligible applicants.</span></h2>
          <p>To be eligible for a grant under this section, an applicant must be a nonprofit organization or a nongovernmental organization and must have experience providing training and technical assistance on how to implement quality parenting initiative principles and practices.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.0962.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>An organization seeking a grant under this section must apply to the commissioner in the time and manner specified by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.0962.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Grant activities.</span></h2>
          <p>Grant money must be used to provide training and technical assistance to county and Tribal agencies, community-based agencies, and other stakeholders on:</p>
          <p>(1) conducting initial foster care telephone calls under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.219#stat.260C.219.6">260C.219, subdivision 6</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) supporting practices that create birth family to foster family partnerships; and</p>
          <p>(3) informing child welfare practices by supporting youth leadership and the participation of individuals with experience in the foster care system.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.0962.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.14.1.0">2023 c 70 art 14 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.36"><b>245.36</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.467">256.467</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.37"><b>245.37</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.468">256.468</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.38"><b>245.38</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.469">256.469</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.39"><b>245.39</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.471">256.471</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.40"><b>245.40</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.472">256.472</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.41"><b>245.41</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.473">256.473</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.42"><b>245.42</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.474">256.474</a>]

      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.43"><b>245.43</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.475">256.475</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.46"><b>245.46</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/650/#laws.21.33.0">1973 c 650 art 21 s 33</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ADULT MENTAL HEALTH ACT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.461">
        <h1 class="shn">245.461 POLICY AND CITATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Citation.</span></h2>
          <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a> may be cited as the "Minnesota Comprehensive Adult Mental Health Act."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Mission statement.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall create and ensure a unified, accountable, comprehensive adult mental health service system that:</p>
          <p>(1) recognizes the right of adults with mental illness to control their own lives as fully as possible;</p>
          <p>(2) promotes the independence and safety of adults with mental illness;</p>
          <p>(3) reduces chronicity of mental illness;</p>
          <p>(4) eliminates abuse of adults with mental illness;</p>
          <p>(5) provides services designed to:</p>
          <p>(i) increase the level of functioning of adults with mental illness or restore them to a previously held higher level of functioning;</p>
          <p>(ii) stabilize adults with mental illness;</p>
          <p>(iii) prevent the development and deepening of mental illness;</p>
          <p>(iv) support and assist adults in resolving mental health problems that impede their functioning;</p>
          <p>(v) promote higher and more satisfying levels of emotional functioning; and</p>
          <p>(vi) promote sound mental health; and</p>
          <p>(6) provides a quality of service that is effective, efficient, appropriate, and consistent with contemporary professional standards in the field of mental health.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/107/#laws.4.22.0">2013 c 107 art 4 s 22</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Housing mission statement.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall ensure that the housing services provided as part of a comprehensive mental health service system:</p>
          <p>(1) allow all persons with mental illness to live in stable, affordable housing, in settings that maximize community integration and opportunities for acceptance;</p>
          <p>(2) allow persons with mental illness to actively participate in the selection of their housing from those living environments available to the general public; and</p>
          <p>(3) provide necessary support regardless of where persons with mental illness choose to live.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Funding from the federal government and other sources.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall seek and apply for federal and other nonstate, nonlocal government funding for the mental health services specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>, in order to maximize nonstate, nonlocal dollars for these services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.461.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Diagnostic codes list.</span></h2>
          <p>By July 1, 2013, the commissioner of human services shall develop a list of diagnostic codes to define the range of child and adult mental illnesses for the statewide mental health system. The commissioner may use the International Classification of Diseases (ICD); the American Psychiatric Association's Diagnostic and Statistical Manual (DSM); or a combination of both to develop the list. The commissioner shall establish an advisory committee, comprising mental health professional associations, counties, tribes, managed care organizations, state agencies, and consumer organizations that shall advise the commissioner regarding development of the diagnostic codes list. The commissioner shall annually notify providers of changes to the list.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.461.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.16.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.1.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/292/#laws.6.1.0">1991 c 292 art 6 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.12.1.0">2012 c 216 art 12 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.462">
        <h1 class="shn">245.462 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4863">245.4863</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Acute care hospital inpatient treatment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Acute care hospital inpatient treatment" means short-term medical, nursing, and psychosocial services provided in an acute care hospital licensed under chapter 144.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Case management services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Case management services" means activities that are coordinated with the community support services program as defined in subdivision 6 and are designed to help adults with serious and persistent mental illness in gaining access to needed medical, social, educational, vocational, and other necessary services as they relate to the client's mental health needs. Case management services include developing a functional assessment, an individual community support plan, referring and assisting the person to obtain needed mental health and other services, ensuring coordination of services, and monitoring the delivery of services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Case management service provider.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Case management service provider" means a case manager or case manager associate employed by the county or other entity authorized by the county board to provide case management services specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4711">245.4711</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A case manager must:</p>
          <p>(1) be skilled in the process of identifying and assessing a wide range of client needs;</p>
          <p>(2) be knowledgeable about local community resources and how to use those resources for the benefit of the client;</p>
          <p>(3) be a mental health practitioner as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.04#stat.245I.04.4">245I.04, subdivision 4</a>, or have a bachelor's degree in one of the behavioral sciences or related fields including, but not limited to, social work, psychology, or nursing from an accredited college or university. A case manager who is not a mental health practitioner and who does not have a bachelor's degree in one of the behavioral sciences or related fields must meet the requirements of paragraph (c); and</p>
          <p>(4) meet the supervision and continuing education requirements described in paragraphs (d), (e), and (f), as applicable.</p>
          <p>(c) Case managers without a bachelor's degree must meet one of the requirements in clauses (1) to (3):</p>
          <p>(1) have three or four years of experience as a case manager associate as defined in this section;</p>
          <p>(2) be a registered nurse without a bachelor's degree and have a combination of specialized training in psychiatry and work experience consisting of community interaction and involvement or community discharge planning in a mental health setting totaling three years; or</p>
          <p>(3) be a person who qualified as a case manager under the 1998 Department of Human Service waiver provision and meet the continuing education and mentoring requirements in this section.</p>
          <p>(d) A case manager with at least 2,000 hours of supervised experience in the delivery of services to adults with mental illness must receive regular ongoing supervision and clinical supervision totaling 38 hours per year of which at least one hour per month must be clinical supervision regarding individual service delivery with a case management supervisor. The remaining 26 hours of supervision may be provided by a case manager with two years of experience. Group supervision may not constitute more than one-half of the required supervision hours. Clinical supervision must be documented in the client record.</p>
          <p>(e) A case manager without 2,000 hours of supervised experience in the delivery of services to adults with mental illness must:</p>
          <p>(1) receive clinical supervision regarding individual service delivery from a mental health professional at least one hour per week until the requirement of 2,000 hours of experience is met; and</p>
          <p>(2) complete 40 hours of training approved by the commissioner in case management skills and the characteristics and needs of adults with serious and persistent mental illness.</p>
          <p>(f) A case manager who is not licensed, registered, or certified by a health-related licensing board must receive 30 hours of continuing education and training in mental illness and mental health services every two years.</p>
          <p>(g) A case manager associate (CMA) must:</p>
          <p>(1) work under the direction of a case manager or case management supervisor;</p>
          <p>(2) be at least 21 years of age;</p>
          <p>(3) have at least a high school diploma or its equivalent; and</p>
          <p>(4) meet one of the following criteria:</p>
          <p>(i) have an associate of arts degree in one of the behavioral sciences or human services;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) be a certified peer specialist under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0615">256B.0615</a>;</p>
          <p>(iii) be a registered nurse without a bachelor's degree;</p>
          <p>(iv) within the previous ten years, have three years of life experience with serious and persistent mental illness as defined in subdivision 20; or as a child had severe emotional disturbance as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4871#stat.245.4871.6">245.4871, subdivision 6</a>; or have three years life experience as a primary caregiver to an adult with serious and persistent mental illness within the previous ten years;</p>
          <p>(v) have 6,000 hours work experience as a nondegreed state hospital technician; or</p>
          <p>(vi) have at least 6,000 hours of supervised experience in the delivery of services to persons with mental illness.</p>
          <p>Individuals meeting one of the criteria in items (i) to (v) may qualify as a case manager after four years of supervised work experience as a case manager associate. Individuals meeting the criteria in item (vi) may qualify as a case manager after three years of supervised experience as a case manager associate.</p>
          <p>(h) A case management associate must meet the following supervision, mentoring, and continuing education requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) have 40 hours of preservice training described under paragraph (e), clause (2);</p>
          <p>(2) receive at least 40 hours of continuing education in mental illness and mental health services annually; and</p>
          <p>(3) receive at least five hours of mentoring per week from a case management mentor.</p>
          <p class="bl">A "case management mentor" means a qualified, practicing case manager or case management supervisor who teaches or advises and provides intensive training and clinical supervision to one or more case manager associates. Mentoring may occur while providing direct services to consumers in the office or in the field and may be provided to individuals or groups of case manager associates. At least two mentoring hours per week must be individual and face-to-face.</p>
          <p>(i) A case management supervisor must meet the criteria for mental health professionals, as specified in subdivision 18.</p>
          <p>(j) An immigrant who does not have the qualifications specified in this subdivision may provide case management services to adult immigrants with serious and persistent mental illness who are members of the same ethnic group as the case manager if the person:</p>
          <p>(1) is currently enrolled in and is actively pursuing credits toward the completion of a bachelor's degree in one of the behavioral sciences or a related field including, but not limited to, social work, psychology, or nursing from an accredited college or university;</p>
          <p>(2) completes 40 hours of training as specified in this subdivision; and</p>
          <p>(3) receives clinical supervision at least once a week until the requirements of this subdivision are met.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.17.113.0">2021 c 30 art 17 s 113</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of human services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Community support services program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Community support services program" means services, other than inpatient or residential treatment services, provided or coordinated by an identified program and staff under the treatment supervision of a mental health professional designed to help adults with serious and persistent mental illness to function and remain in the community. A community support services program includes:</p>
          <p>(1) client outreach,</p>
          <p>(2) medication monitoring,</p>
          <p>(3) assistance in independent living skills,</p>
          <p>(4) development of employability and work-related opportunities,</p>
          <p>(5) crisis assistance,</p>
          <p>(6) psychosocial rehabilitation,</p>
          <p>(7) help in applying for government benefits, and</p>
          <p>(8) housing support services.</p>
          <p>The community support services program must be coordinated with the case management services specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4711">245.4711</a>. A program that meets the accreditation standards for Clubhouse International model programs meets the requirements of this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">County board.</span></h2>
          <p>"County board" means the county board of commissioners or board established pursuant to the Joint Powers Act, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a>, or the Human Services Act, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/402.01">402.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/402.10">402.10</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Day treatment services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Day treatment," "day treatment services," or "day treatment program" means the treatment services described by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0671#stat.256B.0671.3">256B.0671, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Diagnostic assessment.</span></h2>
          <p> "Diagnostic assessment" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.10">245I.10</a>, subdivisions 4 to 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Education and prevention services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Education and prevention services" means services designed to educate the general public or special high-risk target populations about mental illness, to increase the understanding and acceptance of problems associated with mental illness, to increase people's awareness of the availability of resources and services, and to improve people's skills in dealing with high-risk situations known to affect people's mental health and functioning. The services include the distribution of information to individuals and agencies identified by the county board and the local mental health advisory council, on predictors and symptoms of mental disorders, where mental health services are available in the county, and how to access the services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Emergency services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Emergency services" means an immediate response service available on a 24-hour, seven-day-a-week basis for persons having a psychiatric crisis, a mental health crisis, or emergency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.11a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11a.<span class="headnote">Functional assessment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Functional assessment" means an assessment by the case manager of the adult's:</p>
          <p>(1) mental health symptoms as presented in the adult's diagnostic assessment;</p>
          <p>(2) mental health needs as presented in the adult's diagnostic assessment;</p>
          <p>(3) use of drugs and alcohol;</p>
          <p>(4) vocational and educational functioning;</p>
          <p>(5) social functioning, including the use of leisure time;</p>
          <p>(6) interpersonal functioning, including relationships with the adult's family;</p>
          <p>(7) self-care and independent living capacity;</p>
          <p>(8) medical and dental health;</p>
          <p>(9) financial assistance needs;</p>
          <p>(10) housing and transportation needs; and</p>
          <p>(11) other needs and problems.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Individual community support plan.</span></h2>
          <p>"Individual community support plan" means a written plan developed by a case manager on the basis of a diagnostic assessment and functional assessment. The plan identifies specific services needed by an adult with serious and persistent mental illness to develop independence or improved functioning in daily living, health and medication management, social functioning, interpersonal relationships, financial management, housing, transportation, and employment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Individual placement agreement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Individual placement agreement" means a written agreement or supplement to a service contract entered into between the county board and a service provider on behalf of an individual adult to provide residential treatment services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Individual treatment plan.</span></h2>
          <p>"Individual treatment plan" means the formulation of planned services that are responsive to the needs and goals of a client. An individual treatment plan must be completed according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.10">245I.10, subdivisions 7</a> and 8.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.14c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14c.<span class="headnote">Mental health crisis services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Mental health crisis services" means crisis assessment, crisis intervention, and crisis stabilization services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/94/#laws.0.25.0">1991 c 94 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Mental health funds.</span></h2>
          <p>"Mental health funds" are funds expended under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.73">245.73</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256K.10">256K.10</a>, federal mental health block grant funds, and funds expended under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256D.06">256D.06</a> to facilities licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.23">245I.23</a> or Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0500">9520.0500</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0670">9520.0670</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Mental health practitioner.</span></h2>
          <p> "Mental health practitioner" means a staff person who is qualified according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.04#stat.245I.04.4">245I.04, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Mental health professional.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Mental health professional" means a staff person who is qualified according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.04#stat.245I.04.2">245I.04, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Mental health services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Mental health services" means at least all of the treatment services and case management activities that are provided to adults with mental illness and are described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Mental illness.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "Mental illness" means an organic disorder of the brain or a clinically significant disorder of thought, mood, perception, orientation, memory, or behavior that is detailed in a diagnostic codes list published by the commissioner, and that seriously limits a person's capacity to function in primary aspects of daily living such as personal relations, living arrangements, work, and recreation.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An "adult with acute mental illness" means an adult who has a mental illness that is serious enough to require prompt intervention.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For purposes of case management and community support services, a "person with serious and persistent mental illness" means an adult who has a mental illness and meets at least one of the following criteria:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the adult has undergone two or more episodes of inpatient care for a mental illness within the preceding 24 months;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the adult has experienced a continuous psychiatric hospitalization or residential treatment exceeding six months' duration within the preceding 12 months;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the adult has been treated by a crisis team two or more times within the preceding 24 months;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the adult:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) has a diagnosis of schizophrenia, bipolar disorder, major depression, schizoaffective disorder, or borderline personality disorder;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) indicates a significant impairment in functioning; and</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) has a written opinion from a mental health professional, in the last three years, stating that the adult is reasonably likely to have future episodes requiring inpatient or residential treatment, of a frequency described in clause (1) or (2), unless ongoing case management or community support services are provided;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the adult has, in the last three years, been committed by a court as a person who is mentally ill under chapter 253B, or the adult's commitment has been stayed or continued;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the adult (i) was eligible under clauses (1) to (5), but the specified time period has expired or the adult was eligible as a child under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4871#stat.245.4871.6">245.4871, subdivision 6</a>; and (ii) has a written opinion from a mental health professional, in the last three years, stating that the adult is reasonably likely to have future episodes requiring inpatient or residential treatment, of a frequency described in clause (1) or (2), unless ongoing case management or community support services are provided; or</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the adult was eligible as a child under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4871#stat.245.4871.6">245.4871, subdivision 6</a>, and is age 21 or younger.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Outpatient services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Outpatient services" means mental health services, excluding day treatment and community support services programs, provided by or under the treatment supervision of a mental health professional to adults with mental illness who live outside a hospital. Outpatient services include clinical activities such as individual, group, and family therapy; individual treatment planning; diagnostic assessments; medication management; and psychological testing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Regional treatment center inpatient services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Regional treatment center inpatient services" means the 24-hour-a-day comprehensive medical, nursing, or psychosocial services required to be provided pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.12">246C.12</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Residential treatment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Residential treatment" means a 24-hour-a-day program under the treatment supervision of a mental health professional, in a community residential setting other than an acute care hospital or regional treatment center inpatient unit, that must be licensed as a residential treatment program for adults with mental illness under chapter 245I, Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0500">9520.0500</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0670">9520.0670</a>, or other rules adopted by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Service provider.</span></h2>
          <p>"Service provider" means either a county board or an entity under contract with the county board that provides adult mental health services funded by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>. A contracting entity may be an individual, organization, or agency, including a regional treatment center.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.64.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Significant impairment in functioning.</span></h2>
          <p>"Significant impairment in functioning" means a condition, including significant suicidal ideation or thoughts of harming self or others, which harmfully affects, recurrently or consistently, a person's activities of daily living in employment, housing, family and social relationships, or education.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.462.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Treatment supervision.</span></h2>
          <p>"Treatment supervision" means the treatment supervision described by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.06">245I.06</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.462.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

      <div class="section" id="stat.245.463">
        <h1 class="shn">245.463 PLANNING FOR A MENTAL HEALTH SYSTEM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.463.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/107/#laws.4.22.0">2013 c 107 art 4 s 22</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.463.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Technical assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall provide ongoing technical assistance to county boards to improve system capacity and quality. The commissioner and county boards shall exchange information as needed about the numbers of adults with mental illness residing in the county and extent of existing treatment components locally available to serve the needs of those persons. County boards shall cooperate with the commissioner in obtaining necessary planning information upon request.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.463.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/107/#laws.4.22.0">2013 c 107 art 4 s 22</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.463.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/107/#laws.4.22.0">2013 c 107 art 4 s 22</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.463.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.18.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.3.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 3</a>,4; art 6 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/94/#laws.0.24.0">1991 c 94 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.11.11.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 11 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/98/#laws.3.1.0">2005 c 98 art 3 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.464">
        <h1 class="shn">245.464 COORDINATION OF MENTAL HEALTH SYSTEM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.464.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Coordination.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall supervise the development and coordination of locally available adult mental health services by the county boards in a manner consistent with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>. The commissioner shall coordinate locally available services with those services available from the regional treatment center serving the area including state-operated services offered at sites outside of the regional treatment centers. The commissioner shall provide technical assistance to county boards in developing and maintaining locally available mental health services. The commissioner shall monitor the county board's progress in developing its full system capacity and quality through ongoing review of the county board's adult mental health component of the community social services plan and other information as required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.464.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Priorities.</span></h2>
          <p>By January 1, 1990, the commissioner shall require that each of the treatment services and management activities described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.469">245.469</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.477">245.477</a> are developed for adults with mental illness within available resources based on the following ranked priorities:</p>
          <p>(1) the provision of locally available emergency services;</p>
          <p>(2) the provision of locally available services to all adults with serious and persistent mental illness and all adults with acute mental illness;</p>
          <p>(3) the provision of specialized services regionally available to meet the special needs of all adults with serious and persistent mental illness and all adults with acute mental illness;</p>
          <p>(4) the provision of locally available services to adults with other mental illness; and</p>
          <p>(5) the provision of education and preventive mental health services targeted at high-risk populations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.464.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Public-private partnerships.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may establish a mechanism by which counties, the Department of Human Services, Direct Care and Treatment, hospitals, health plans, consumers, providers, and others may enter into agreements that allow for capacity building and oversight of any agreed-upon entity that is developed through these partnerships. The purpose of these partnerships is the development and provision of mental health services which would be more effective, efficient, and accessible than services that might be provided separately by each partner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.464.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.19.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.5.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/94/#laws.0.24.0">1991 c 94 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.7.4.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 7 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.3.8.0">2004 c 288 art 3 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/98/#laws.3.2.0">2005 c 98 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.8.5.0">2024 c 79 art 8 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/125/#laws.5.43.0">2024 c 125 art 5 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.50.43.0">2024 c 127 art 50 s 43</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.465">
        <h1 class="shn">245.465 DUTIES OF COUNTY BOARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.465.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Use of mental health funds.</span></h2>
          <p>The county board in each county shall use its share of mental health funds allocated by the commissioner according to the mental health plan approved by the commissioner. The county board must:</p>
          <p>(1) develop and coordinate a system of affordable and locally available adult mental health services in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) with the involvement of the local adult mental health advisory council or the adult mental health subcommittee of an existing advisory council, develop a biennial adult mental health plan which considers the assessment of unmet needs in the county as reported by the local adult mental health advisory council under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.466#stat.245.466.5">245.466, subdivision 5</a>, clause (3). The county shall provide, upon request of the local adult mental health advisory council, readily available data to assist in the determination of unmet needs;</p>
          <p>(3) provide for case management services to adults with serious and persistent mental illness in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462">245.462</a>, subdivisions 3 and 4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4711">245.4711</a>; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) provide for screening of adults specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.476">245.476</a> upon admission to a residential treatment facility or acute care hospital inpatient, or informal admission to a regional treatment center;</p>
          <p>(5) prudently administer grants and purchase-of-service contracts that the county board determines are necessary to fulfill its responsibilities under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) assure that mental health professionals, mental health practitioners, and case managers employed by or under contract with the county to provide mental health services have experience and training in working with adults with mental illness.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.465.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/282/#laws.16.17.0">2006 c 282 art 16 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.465.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Responsibility not duplicated.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For individuals who have health care coverage, the county board is not responsible for providing mental health services which are within the limits of the individual's health care coverage.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.465.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.20.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.74.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.6.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/94/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 94 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/292/#laws.4.4.0">1991 c 292 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.11.11.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 11 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/98/#laws.3.3.0">2005 c 98 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.8.4.0">2007 c 147 art 8 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.466">
        <h1 class="shn">245.466 LOCAL SERVICE DELIVERY SYSTEM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Development of services.</span></h2>
          <p>The county board in each county is responsible for using all available resources to develop and coordinate a system of locally available and affordable adult mental health services. The county board may provide some or all of the mental health services and activities specified in subdivision 2 directly through a county agency or under contracts with other individuals or agencies. A county or counties may enter into an agreement with a regional treatment center under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246.57">246.57</a> or with any state facility or program as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246.50#stat.246.50.3">246.50, subdivision 3</a>, under the control of the Direct Care and Treatment executive board to enable the county or counties to provide the treatment services in subdivision 2. Services provided through an agreement between a county and a regional treatment center must meet the same requirements as services from other service providers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Adult mental health services.</span></h2>
          <p>The adult mental health service system developed by each county board must include the following services:</p>
          <p>(1) education and prevention services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.468">245.468</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) emergency services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.469">245.469</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) outpatient services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.470">245.470</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) community support program services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4711">245.4711</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) residential treatment services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.472">245.472</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) acute care hospital inpatient treatment services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.473">245.473</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) regional treatment center inpatient services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/246C.12">246C.12</a>;</p>
          <p>(8) screening in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.476">245.476</a>; and</p>
          <p>(9) case management in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.3">245.462, subdivision 3</a>; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4711">245.4711</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Local contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>Effective January 1, 1988, the county board shall review all proposed county agreements, grants, or other contracts related to mental health services for funding from any local, state, or federal governmental sources. Contracts with service providers must:</p>
          <p>(1) name the commissioner as a third-party beneficiary;</p>
          <p>(2) identify monitoring and evaluation procedures not in violation of the Minnesota Government Data Practices Act, chapter 13, which are necessary to ensure effective delivery of quality services;</p>
          <p>(3) include a provision that makes payments conditional on compliance by the contractor and all subcontractors with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a> and all other applicable laws, rules, and standards; and</p>
          <p>(4) require financial controls and auditing procedures.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Transition plan related to termination of contract.</span></h2>
          <p>Counties must prepare a transition plan that provides for continuity of care in the event of contract termination with a community mental health center under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.62">245.62</a>, or a community support services program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.6">245.462, subdivision 6</a>. The county shall provide at least 90 days' notice of the termination to the contracted agency and the commissioner of human services. The transition plan must provide information to clients on how to access medical records and how to transfer to other providers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Joint county mental health agreements.</span></h2>
          <p>In order to provide efficiently the services required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>, counties are encouraged to join with one or more county boards to establish a multicounty local mental health authority pursuant to the Joint Powers Act, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a>, the Human Services Act, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/402.01">402.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/402.10">402.10</a>, community mental health center provisions, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.62">245.62</a>, or enter into multicounty mental health agreements. Participating county boards shall establish acceptable ways of apportioning the cost of the services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Local advisory council.</span></h2>
          <p>The county board, individually or in conjunction with other county boards, shall establish a local adult mental health advisory council or mental health subcommittee of an existing advisory council. The council's members must reflect a broad range of community interests. They must include at least one consumer, one family member of an adult with mental illness, one mental health professional, and one community support services program representative. The local adult mental health advisory council or mental health subcommittee of an existing advisory council shall meet at least quarterly to review, evaluate, and make recommendations regarding the local mental health system. Annually, the local adult mental health advisory council or mental health subcommittee of an existing advisory council shall:</p>
          <p>(1) arrange for input from the regional treatment center's mental illness program unit regarding coordination of care between the regional treatment center and community-based services;</p>
          <p>(2) identify for the county board the individuals, providers, agencies, and associations as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.10">245.462, subdivision 10</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) provide to the county board a report of unmet mental health needs of adults residing in the county to be included in the county's mental health plan, and participate in developing the mental health plan; and</p>
          <p>(4) coordinate its review, evaluation, and recommendations regarding the local mental health system with the state advisory council on mental health.</p>
          <p>The county board shall consider the advice of its local mental health advisory council or mental health subcommittee of an existing advisory council in carrying out its authorities and responsibilities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Other local authority.</span></h2>
          <p>The county board may establish procedures and policies that are not contrary to those of the commissioner or sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a> regarding local adult mental health services and facilities. The county board shall perform other acts necessary to carry out sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.466.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">IMD downsizing flexibility.</span></h2>
          <p> If a county presents a budget-neutral plan for a net reduction in the number of institution for mental disease (IMD) beds funded by housing support under chapter 256I, the commissioner may transfer the net savings from housing support to medical assistance and mental health grants to provide appropriate services in non-IMD settings. For the purposes of this subdivision, "a budget neutral plan" means a plan that does not increase the state share of costs.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.466.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.21.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.75.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 75</a>-77; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.4.7.0">1989 c 282 art 4 s 7</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/94/#laws.0.2.0">1991 c 94 s 2</a>,24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.7.5.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 7 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/107/#laws.0.2.0">1997 c 107 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/86/#laws.1.56.0">1999 c 86 art 1 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.11.11.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 11 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/10/#laws.1.42.0">2005 c 10 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/98/#laws.3.4.0">2005 c 98 art 3 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.29.1.0">2014 c 312 art 29 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/21/#laws.1.38.0">2015 c 21 art 1 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.83.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 83</a>; art 2 s 44; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.2.39.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 2 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.8.6.0">2024 c 79 art 8 s 6</a>; art 10 s 2</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.4661">
        <h1 class="shn">245.4661 ADULT MENTAL HEALTH INITIATIVE SERVICES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.4661.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Adult mental health initiative services.</span></h2>
          <p>Each county board, county boards acting jointly, or tribal government must provide or contract for sufficient infrastructure for the delivery of mental health services under the Minnesota Comprehensive Adult Mental Health Act, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461">245.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.486">245.486</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.4661.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Program design and implementation.</span></h2>
          <p> Adult mental health initiatives shall be responsible for designing, planning, improving, and maintaining a mental health service delivery system for adults with serious and persistent mental illness that would:</p>
          <p>(1) provide an expanded array of services from which clients can choose services appropriate to their needs;</p>
          <p>(2) be based on purchasing strategies that improve access and coordinate services without cost shifting;</p>
          <p>(3) prioritize evidence-based services and implement services that are promising practices or theory-based practices so that the service can be evaluated according to subdivision 5a;</p>
          <p>(4) incorporate existing state facilities and resources into the community mental health infrastructure through creative partnerships with local vendors; and</p>
          <p>(5) utilize existing categorical funding streams and reimbursement sources in combined and creative ways, except appropriations and all funds that are attributable to the operation of state-operated services under the control of the Direct Care and Treatment executive board are excluded unless appropriated specifically by the legislature for a purpose consistent with this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.4661.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Adult mental health initiative evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p>Evaluation of each adult mental health initiative will be based on outcome evaluation criteria determined by the commissioners of human services and management and budget after consultation with stakeholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.4661.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Notice of adult mental health initiative discontinuation.</span></h2>
          <p>Each adult mental health initiative may be discontinued for any reason by the managing entity or the commissioner of human services, after 90 days' written notice to the other party.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.4661.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Planning for adult mental health initiatives.</span></h2>
          <p> Each local plan for adult mental health initiative services must be developed under the direction of the county board, or multiple county boards acting jointly, as the local mental health authority.
