The ombudsperson may delegate to staff members any authority or duties of the office, except the duty to provide reports to the governor, commissioner, or the legislature.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Access to records.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The ombudsperson or designee, excluding volunteers, has access to any data of a state agency necessary for the discharge of the ombudsperson's duties, including records classified as confidential data on individuals or private data on individuals under chapter 13 or any other law. The ombudsperson's data request must relate to a specific case and is subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.03#stat.13.03.4">13.03, subdivision 4</a>. If the data concerns an individual, the ombudsperson or designee shall first obtain the individual's consent. If the individual is unable to consent and has no parent or legal guardian, then the ombudsperson's or designee's access to the data is authorized by this section.</p>
          <p>(b) The ombudsperson and designees must adhere to the Minnesota Government Data Practices Act and must not disseminate any private or confidential data on individuals unless specifically authorized by state, local, or federal law or pursuant to a court order.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner of human services; the commissioner of children, youth, and families; and any county agency must provide the ombudsperson copies of all fix-it tickets, correction orders, and licensing actions issued to family child care providers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Independence of action.</span></h2>
          <p>In carrying out the duties under this section, the ombudsperson may, independently of the department, provide testimony to the legislature, make periodic reports to the legislature, and address areas of concern to family child care providers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Civil actions.</span></h2>
          <p>The ombudsperson or designee is not civilly liable for any action taken under this section if the action was taken in good faith, was within the scope of the ombudsperson's authority, and did not constitute willful or reckless misconduct.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Qualifications.</span></h2>
          <p>The ombudsperson must be a person who has knowledge and experience concerning the provision of family child care. The ombudsperson must be experienced in dealing with governmental entities, interpretation of laws and regulations, investigations, record keeping, report writing, public speaking, and management. A person is not eligible to serve as the ombudsperson while running for or holding public office or while holding a family child care license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.47.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.975.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Posting.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of children, youth, and families shall post on the department's website the mailing address, email address, and telephone number for the office of the ombudsperson. The commissioner shall provide family child care providers with the mailing address, email address, and telephone number of the ombudsperson's office on the family child care licensing website and upon request of a family child care applicant or provider. Counties must provide family child care applicants and providers with the name, mailing address, email address, and telephone number of the ombudsperson's office upon request.</p>
          <p>(b) The ombudsperson must approve all postings and notices required by the department and counties under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.975.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.3.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.5.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 5</a>-7</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">COMPULSIVE GAMBLING</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.98">
        <h1 class="shn">245.98 COMPULSIVE GAMBLING TREATMENT PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, "compulsive gambler" means a person who is chronically and progressively preoccupied with gambling and with the urge to gamble to the extent that the gambling behavior compromises, disrupts, or damages personal, family, or vocational pursuits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Program.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner of human services shall establish a program for the treatment of compulsive gamblers. The commissioner may contract with an entity with expertise regarding the treatment of compulsive gambling to operate the program. The program may include the establishment of a statewide toll-free number, resource library, public education programs; regional in-service training programs and conferences for health care professionals, educators, treatment providers, employee assistance programs, and criminal justice representatives; and the establishment of certification standards for programs and service providers. The commissioner may enter into agreements with other entities and may employ or contract with consultants to facilitate the provision of these services or the training of individuals to qualify them to provide these services. The program may also include inpatient and outpatient treatment and rehabilitation services for residents in different settings, including a temporary or permanent residential setting for mental health or substance use disorder, and individuals in jails or correctional facilities. The program may also include research studies. The research studies must include baseline and prevalence studies for adolescents and adults to identify those at the highest risk. The program must be approved by the commissioner before it is established.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Assessment of certain offenders.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt by rule criteria to be used in conducting compulsive gambling assessments of offenders under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.115#stat.609.115.9">609.115, subdivision 9</a>. The commissioner shall also adopt by rule standards to qualify a person to: (1) assess offenders for compulsive gambling treatment; and (2) provide treatment indicated in a compulsive gambling assessment. The rules must specify the circumstances in which, in the absence of an independent assessor, the assessment may be performed by a person with a direct or shared financial interest or referral relationship resulting in shared financial gain with a treatment provider.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/207/#laws.11.12.0">1995 c 207 art 11 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Contribution by tribal gaming.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services is authorized to enter into an agreement with the governing body of any Indian tribe located within the boundaries of the state of Minnesota that conducts either class II or class III gambling, as defined in section 4 of the Indian Gaming Regulatory Act, Public Law 100-497, and future amendments to it, for the purpose of obtaining funding for compulsive gambling programs from the Indian tribe. Prior to entering into any agreement with an Indian tribe under this section, the commissioner shall consult with and obtain the approval of the governor or governor's designated representatives authorized to negotiate a tribal-state compact regulating the conduct of class III gambling on Indian lands of a tribe requesting negotiations. Contributions collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner of human services for the compulsive gambling treatment program under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.98.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Standards.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall create standards for treatment and provider qualifications for the treatment component of the compulsive gambling program.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.98.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/334/#laws.7.1.0">1989 c 334 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/336/#laws.2.7.0">1991 c 336 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/146/#laws.3.7.0">1993 c 146 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/86/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 86 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/203/#laws.9.3.0">1997 c 203 art 9 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.8.10.0">2007 c 147 art 8 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.4.51.0">2022 c 98 art 4 s 51</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245.981"><b>245.981</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.14.33.0">2022 c 98 art 14 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.982">
        <h1 class="shn">245.982 PROGRAM SUPPORT.</h1>
        <p>In order to address the problem of gambling in this state, the compulsive gambling fund should attempt to assess the beneficiaries of gambling, on a percentage basis according to the revenue they receive from gambling, for the costs of programs to help problem gamblers and their families. In that light, the governor is requested to contact the chairs of the 11 tribal governments in this state and request a contribution of funds for the compulsive gambling program. The governor should seek a total supplemental contribution of $643,000. Funds received from the tribal governments in this state shall be deposited in the Indian gaming revolving account.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.982.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/407/#laws.8.5.0">1998 c 407 art 8 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MENTAL ILLNESS CRISIS HOUSING</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.99">
        <h1 class="shn">245.99 ADULT MENTAL ILLNESS CRISIS HOUSING ASSISTANCE PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.99.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation.</span></h2>
          <p>The adult mental illness crisis housing assistance program is established in the Department of Human Services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.99.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Rental assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>The program shall pay up to 90 days of housing assistance for persons with a serious mental illness who require inpatient or residential care for stabilization. The commissioner of human services may extend the length of assistance on a case-by-case basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.99.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p> Housing assistance under this section is available only to persons of low or moderate income as determined by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.99.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Administration of crisis housing assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may contract with organizations or government units experienced in housing assistance to operate the program under this section. This program is not an entitlement. The commissioner may take any of the following steps whenever the commissioner projects that funds will be inadequate to meet demand in a given fiscal year:</p>
          <p>(1) transfer funds from mental health grants in the same appropriation; and</p>
          <p>(2) impose statewide restrictions as to the type and amount of assistance available to each recipient under this program, including reducing the income eligibility level, limiting reimbursement to a percentage of each recipient's costs, limiting housing assistance to 60 days per recipient, or closing the program for the remainder of the fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.99.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/245/#laws.4.8.0">1999 c 245 art 4 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.9.19.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 9 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/379/#laws.1.113.0">2002 c 379 art 1 s 113</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.16.3.0">2016 c 189 art 16 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.991">
        <h1 class="shn">245.991 PROJECTS FOR ASSISTANCE IN TRANSITION FROM HOMELESSNESS PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.991.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services must establish the projects for assistance in transition from homelessness program to prevent or end homelessness for people with serious mental illness, substance use disorder, or co-occurring substance use disorder and ensure the commissioner achieves the goals of the housing mission statement in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461#stat.245.461.4">245.461, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.991.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligible applicants.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant for a grant under this section must be a nonprofit organization, county, or other entity that provides services to help individuals transition from homelessness.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.991.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Allowable grant activities.</span></h2>
          <p>Grantees must provide homeless outreach and case management services. Projects may provide clinical assessment, habilitation and rehabilitation services, community mental health services, substance use disorder treatment, housing transition and sustaining services, or direct assistance funding. Services must be provided to individuals with a serious mental illness, or with a co-occurring substance use disorder, and who are homeless or at imminent risk of homelessness. Individuals receiving homeless outreach services may be presumed eligible until a serious mental illness can be verified.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.991.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Outcomes.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Grantees must submit an annual report to the commissioner for the purposes of evaluating the effectiveness of the grant program. The report must include:</p>
          <p>(1) the number of individuals to whom the grantee provided homeless outreach services;</p>
          <p>(2) the number of individuals the grantee enrolled in case management services;</p>
          <p>(3) the number of individuals that were able to access mental health and substance use disorder treatment services; and</p>
          <p>(4) the number of individuals that were able to transition from homelessness to housing.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must submit the results of the evaluation to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees with jurisdiction over mental health and homelessness.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.991.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Federal aid or grants.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services must comply with all conditions and requirements necessary to receive federal aid or grants with respect to homeless services or programs as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.70">245.70</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.991.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.7.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.66.12.0">2024 c 127 art 66 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245.992">
        <h1 class="shn">245.992 HOUSING WITH SUPPORT FOR ADULTS WITH SERIOUS MENTAL ILLNESS PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.992.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services must establish a housing with support for adults with serious mental illness program to prevent or end homelessness for people with serious mental illness, to increase the availability of housing with support, and to ensure the commissioner may achieve the goals of the housing mission statement in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.461#stat.245.461.4">245.461, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.992.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligible beneficiaries.</span></h2>
          <p>Program activities must be provided to people with a serious mental illness, or with a co-occurring substance use disorder, who meet homeless criteria determined by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.992.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Allowable grant activities.</span></h2>
          <p>Grantees must provide a range of activities and supportive services that ensure individuals obtain and retain permanent supportive housing. Program activities may include case management, site-based housing services, housing transition and sustaining services, outreach services, community support services, or direct assistance funding.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245.992.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Outcomes.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Grantees must submit an annual report to the commissioner for the purposes of evaluating the effectiveness of the grant program. The report must include:</p>
          <p>(1) whether the grantee's housing and activities utilized evidence-based practices;</p>
          <p>(2) the number of individuals that were able to transition from homelessness to housing;</p>
          <p>(3) the number of individuals that were able to retain housing; and</p>
          <p>(4) whether the individuals were satisfied with their housing.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must submit the results of the evaluation to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees with jurisdiction over mental health and homelessness.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245.992.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.8.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
